To address this gap, this work investigates the shape reconstruction abilities of a third-order strain interpolation method, examining its ability to capture both individual and combined deformation effects. These results are compared and discussed against the Geometric-Variable Strain approach. Subsequently, simulation results are experimentally verified by reshaping a slender rod while recording the resulting configurations using cameras. The rod configuration is imposed using a manipulator displacing one of its tips and extracted through reflective markers, without the aid of any other external sensor -- i.e. strain gauges or wrench sensors placed along the rod. The experiments demonstrate good agreement between the model predictions and observed shapes, with average error of 0.58% of the rod length and average computational time of 0.32s per configuration, outperforming existing models.

</details>


### [115] [GraspLDP: Towards Generalizable Grasping Policy via Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22862)
*Enda Xiang,Haoxiang Ma,Xinzhu Ma,Zicheng Liu,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合抓取先验知识的扩散策略方法，通过潜在扩散策略引导动作块解码，并引入自监督重建目标嵌入抓取性先验，显著提升模仿学习抓取策略的精度和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿学习的抓取策略存在执行不精确、空间泛化能力有限和物体泛化能力差的问题。扩散策略方法已成为机器人操作任务的主流方法，但抓取作为操作的关键子任务，其精度和泛化能力需要特别关注。

Method: 1. 将抓取先验知识融入扩散策略框架；2. 使用潜在扩散策略通过抓取姿态先验引导动作块解码，确保生成的运动轨迹紧密遵循可行的抓取配置；3. 在扩散过程中引入自监督重建目标来嵌入抓取性先验：在每个反向扩散步骤中，从中间表示重建回投影抓取性的手腕相机图像。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验均表明，该方法显著优于基线方法，并展现出强大的动态抓取能力。

Conclusion: 通过将抓取先验知识融入扩散策略框架，并引入自监督重建目标，能够有效提升模仿学习抓取策略的精度和泛化能力，为解决现有方法的局限性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on enhancing the grasping precision and generalization of manipulation policies learned via imitation learning. Diffusion-based policy learning methods have recently become the mainstream approach for robotic manipulation tasks. As grasping is a critical subtask in manipulation, the ability of imitation-learned policies to execute precise and generalizable grasps merits particular attention. Existing imitation learning techniques for grasping often suffer from imprecise grasp executions, limited spatial generalization, and poor object generalization. To address these challenges, we incorporate grasp prior knowledge into the diffusion policy framework. In particular, we employ a latent diffusion policy to guide action chunk decoding with grasp pose prior, ensuring that generated motion trajectories adhere closely to feasible grasp configurations. Furthermore, we introduce a self-supervised reconstruction objective during diffusion to embed the graspness prior: at each reverse diffusion step, we reconstruct wrist-camera images back-projected the graspness from the intermediate representations. Both simulation and real robot experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baseline methods and exhibits strong dynamic grasping capabilities.

</details>


### [116] [DySL-VLA: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model Inference via Dynamic-Static Layer-Skipping for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22896)
*Zebin Yang,Yijiahao Qi,Tong Xie,Bo Yu,Shaoshan Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DySL-VLA：一种通过动态跳过VLA层来降低计算成本的框架，根据动作重要性选择性执行，在保持精度的同时实现3.75倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人任务中表现出色，但高计算成本阻碍了实时应用。观察到任务中不同动作的重要性不同：关键步骤需要高精度，次要步骤可容忍更大方差。

Method: 提出DySL-VLA框架，将VLA层分为信息层（始终执行）和增量层（可选择性跳过）。采用先验-后验跳过指导机制决定何时启动层跳过，并使用跳过感知的两阶段知识蒸馏算法训练。

Result: 在Calvin数据集上，相比Deer-VLA提升2.1%的成功长度，可训练参数减少85.7倍，在同等精度下相比RoboFlamingo基线实现3.75倍加速。

Conclusion: DySL-VLA通过动态层跳过机制有效平衡了VLA模型的精度和效率，为实时机器人应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable success in robotic tasks like manipulation by fusing a language model's reasoning with a vision model's 3D understanding. However, their high computational cost remains a major obstacle for real-world applications that require real-time performance. We observe that the actions within a task have varying levels of importance: critical steps demand high precision, while less important ones can tolerate more variance. Leveraging this insight, we propose DySL-VLA, a novel framework that addresses computational cost by dynamically skipping VLA layers based on each action's importance. DySL-VLA categorizes its layers into two types: informative layers, which are consistently executed, and incremental layers, which can be selectively skipped. To intelligently skip layers without sacrificing accuracy, we invent a prior-post skipping guidance mechanism to determine when to initiate layer-skipping. We also propose a skip-aware two-stage knowledge distillation algorithm to efficiently train a standard VLA into a DySL-VLA. Our experiments indicate that DySL-VLA achieves 2.1% improvement in success length over Deer-VLA on the Calvin dataset, while simultaneously reducing trainable parameters by a factor of 85.7 and providing a 3.75x speedup relative to the RoboFlamingo baseline at iso-accuracy. Our code is available on https://github.com/PKU-SEC-Lab/DYSL_VLA.

</details>


### [117] [Bayesian Preference Elicitation: Human-In-The-Loop Optimization of An Active Prosthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22922)
*Sophia Taddei,Wouter Koppen,Eligia Alfio,Stefano Nuzzo,Louis Flynn,Maria Alejandra Diaz,Sebastian Rojas Gonzalez,Tom Dhaene,Kevin De Pauw,Ivo Couckuyt,Tom Verstraten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于人机协同优化(HILO)的方法，利用用户直接偏好来高效个性化调整四参数假肢控制器，通过偏好驱动的多目标贝叶斯优化实现用户中心的假肢控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为截肢者调整主动假肢的过程耗时且依赖的指标可能无法完全反映用户需求，需要更高效、用户中心的个性化调整方法。

Method: 采用人机协同优化(HILO)方法，结合偏好驱动的多目标贝叶斯优化，使用专门为偏好学习设计的先进采集函数，包含离散版本(EUBO-LineCoSpar)和连续版本(BPE4Prost)两种算法变体。

Result: 在基准函数和实际应用试验中展示了高效收敛、稳健的偏好获取以及可测量的生物力学改进，证明了偏好驱动调整在用户中心假肢控制中的潜力。

Conclusion: 偏好驱动的调整方法能够有效实现用户中心的假肢控制个性化，提高调整效率并更好地满足用户需求，为假肢控制优化提供了有前景的新途径。

Abstract: Tuning active prostheses for people with amputation is time-consuming and relies on metrics that may not fully reflect user needs. We introduce a human-in-the-loop optimization (HILO) approach that leverages direct user preferences to personalize a standard four-parameter prosthesis controller efficiently. Our method employs preference-based Multiobjective Bayesian Optimization that uses a state-or-the-art acquisition function especially designed for preference learning, and includes two algorithmic variants: a discrete version (\textit{EUBO-LineCoSpar}), and a continuous version (\textit{BPE4Prost}). Simulation results on benchmark functions and real-application trials demonstrate efficient convergence, robust preference elicitation, and measurable biomechanical improvements, illustrating the potential of preference-driven tuning for user-centered prosthesis control.

</details>


### [118] [Considering Perspectives for Automated Driving Ethics: Collective Risk in Vehicular Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22940)
*Leon Tolksdorf,Arturo Tejada,Christian Birkner,Nathan van de Wouw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 自动驾驶车辆仅从自身视角最小化风险可能增加其他道路使用者的风险，需要采用集体风险最小化策略来平衡所有道路使用者的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶车辆的运动规划策略仅从AV自身视角最小化风险，未考虑其他道路使用者的风险视角，这可能导致AV的决策对其他道路使用者不道德，且可能增加他们的风险。

Method: 提出一种支持在所有道路使用者视角之间切换风险最小化策略的AV运动规划策略，通过集体风险视角平衡所有道路使用者的风险。

Result: 研究发现其他道路使用者的风险视角通常与AV自身视角不同；采用集体风险最小化策略的AV能最好地降低整体交通风险，同时为他人利益承担略高自身风险；该策略还能通过自信行动提高AV的出行效率。

Conclusion: 为实现包含AV的道路交通中的道德性，必须在AV决策中考虑每个道路使用者的风险视角，这种自我反思行为是社会可接受AV行为的自然前提。

Abstract: Recent automated vehicle (AV) motion planning strategies evolve around minimizing risk in road traffic. However, they exclusively consider risk from the AV's perspective and, as such, do not address the ethicality of its decisions for other road users. We argue that this does not reduce the risk of each road user, as risk may be different from the perspective of each road user. Indeed, minimizing the risk from the AV's perspective may not imply that the risk from the perspective of other road users is also being minimized; in fact, it may even increase. To test this hypothesis, we propose an AV motion planning strategy that supports switching risk minimization strategies between all road user perspectives. We find that the risk from the perspective of other road users can generally be considered different to the risk from the AV's perspective. Taking a collective risk perspective, i.e., balancing the risks of all road users, we observe an AV that minimizes overall traffic risk the best, while putting itself at slightly higher risk for the benefit of others, which is consistent with human driving behavior. In addition, adopting a collective risk minimization strategy can also be beneficial to the AV's travel efficiency by acting assertively when other road users maintain a low risk estimate of the AV. Yet, the AV drives conservatively when its planned actions are less predictable to other road users, i.e., associated with high risk. We argue that such behavior is a form of self-reflection and a natural prerequisite for socially acceptable AV behavior. We conclude that to facilitate ethicality in road traffic that includes AVs, the risk-perspective of each road user must be considered in the decision-making of AVs.

</details>


### [119] [Automated Robotic Needle Puncture for Percutaneous Dilatational Tracheostomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22952)
*Yuan Tang,Bruno V. Adorno,Brendan A. McGrath,Andrew Weightman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于经皮扩张气管切开术（PDT）的机器人辅助穿刺系统，通过电磁传感器引导和自适应约束控制器实现自动化针头插入，显著提高了穿刺精度和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDT是重症监护室常用手术，但当前的手动穿刺存在较大位置和角度误差（5mm和30度），可能导致严重并发症如大出血和后壁穿孔。需要提高穿刺精度和降低手术难度。

Method: 使用速度控制机器人操纵器自动化针头插入，通过两个电磁传感器（针尖和气管内）提供姿态数据，采用自适应约束控制器在线适应不确定运动学参数并避免与患者身体和组织碰撞。

Result: 在人体模型上进行400次PDT穿刺测试，绝对中位穿刺位置误差为1.7mm（IQR：1.9mm），中线偏差为4.13度（IQR：4.55度）。仿真验证了控制器实现，并提供了无碰撞插入的正式保证。

Conclusion: 机器人PDT穿刺系统在模拟实验设置中显示出较小的偏差，并提供了无碰撞插入的正式保证，表明机器人辅助PDT穿刺的可行性，有望提高手术精度和安全性。

Abstract: Percutaneous dilatational tracheostomy (PDT) is frequently performed on patients in intensive care units for prolonged mechanical ventilation. The needle puncture, as the most critical step of PDT, could lead to adverse consequences such as major bleeding and posterior tracheal wall perforation if performed inaccurately. Current practices of PDT puncture are all performed manually with no navigation assistance, which leads to large position and angular errors (5 mm and 30 degree). To improve the accuracy and reduce the difficulty of the PDT procedure, we propose a system that automates the needle insertion using a velocity-controlled robotic manipulator. Guided using pose data from two electromagnetic sensors, one at the needle tip and the other inside the trachea, the robotic system uses an adaptive constrained controller to adapt the uncertain kinematic parameters online and avoid collisions with the patient's body and tissues near the target. Simulations were performed to validate the controller's implementation, and then four hundred PDT punctures were performed on a mannequin to evaluate the position and angular accuracy. The absolute median puncture position error was 1.7 mm (IQR: 1.9 mm) and midline deviation was 4.13 degree (IQR: 4.55 degree), measured by the sensor inside the trachea. The small deviations from the nominal puncture in a simulated experimental setup and formal guarantees of collision-free insertions suggest the feasibility of the robotic PDT puncture.

</details>


### [120] [DigiArm: An Anthropomorphic 3D-Printed Prosthetic Hand with Enhanced Dexterity for Typing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23017)
*Dean Zadok,Tom Naamani,Yuval Bar-Ratson,Elisha Barash,Oren Salzman,Alon Wolf,Alex M. Bronstein,Nili Krausz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本、轻量化的3D打印机器人假肢手，专门针对电子设备交互（如键盘打字、钢琴演奏）进行优化设计，提升精细操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假肢手在灵巧性和直观控制方面存在局限，商业假肢缺乏精细手指运动所需的精度，无法满足电子设备交互等精细操作需求，需要可访问、可复制的假肢设计。

Method: 设计低成本、轻量化的3D打印机器人假肢手，包含手指外展/内收间距调节机制、优化打字功能的2-D腕关节（尺骨/桡骨偏转控制）以及独立手指按压控制。

Result: 通过研究展示参与者能够使用该机器人假肢手实时执行键盘打字和钢琴演奏任务，支持不同水平的手指和手腕运动，证明设计能更有效地执行关键打字动作。

Conclusion: 提出的设计能够增强假肢手的功能性，特别是在电子设备交互和精细操作方面，为提升假肢手的灵巧性和实用性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Despite recent advancements, existing prosthetic limbs are unable to replicate the dexterity and intuitive control of the human hand. Current control systems for prosthetic hands are often limited to grasping, and commercial prosthetic hands lack the precision needed for dexterous manipulation or applications that require fine finger motions. Thus, there is a critical need for accessible and replicable prosthetic designs that enable individuals to interact with electronic devices and perform precise finger pressing, such as keyboard typing or piano playing, while preserving current prosthetic capabilities. This paper presents a low-cost, lightweight, 3D-printed robotic prosthetic hand, specifically engineered for enhanced dexterity with electronic devices such as a computer keyboard or piano, as well as general object manipulation. The robotic hand features a mechanism to adjust finger abduction/adduction spacing, a 2-D wrist with the inclusion of controlled ulnar/radial deviation optimized for typing, and control of independent finger pressing. We conducted a study to demonstrate how participants can use the robotic hand to perform keyboard typing and piano playing in real time, with different levels of finger and wrist motion. This supports the notion that our proposed design can allow for the execution of key typing motions more effectively than before, aiming to enhance the functionality of prosthetic hands.

</details>


### [121] [An Empirical Analysis of Cooperative Perception for Occlusion Risk Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23051)
*Aihong Wang,Tenghui Xie,Fuxi Wen,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Risk of Tracking Loss (RTL)风险度量，用于评估遮挡风险，分析V2X部署策略，并提出非对称通信框架提升风险缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡对自动驾驶车辆构成重大挑战，传统风险度量难以捕捉随时间累积的风险特性，需要更全面的风险评估方法。

Method: 提出RTL风险度量，聚合遮挡期间的瞬时风险强度；进行大规模统计分析；提出非对称V2X通信框架，允许非连接车辆接收警告。

Result: RTL能有效表征遮挡风险；V2X完全渗透理论上消除风险，但需要75-90%高渗透率才有显著统计效益；非对称框架在25%渗透率下优于传统对称模型75%渗透率，效益在50%渗透率时饱和。

Conclusion: RTL提供了关键风险评估度量，非对称V2X通信框架为加速V2X部署的安全效益提供了成本有效的战略路线图。

Abstract: Occlusions present a significant challenge for connected and automated vehicles, as they can obscure critical road users from perception systems. Traditional risk metrics often fail to capture the cumulative nature of these threats over time adequately. In this paper, we propose a novel and universal risk assessment metric, the Risk of Tracking Loss (RTL), which aggregates instantaneous risk intensity throughout occluded periods. This provides a holistic risk profile that encompasses both high-intensity, short-term threats and prolonged exposure. Utilizing diverse and high-fidelity real-world datasets, a large-scale statistical analysis is conducted to characterize occlusion risk and validate the effectiveness of the proposed metric. The metric is applied to evaluate different vehicle-to-everything (V2X) deployment strategies. Our study shows that full V2X penetration theoretically eliminates this risk, the reduction is highly nonlinear; a substantial statistical benefit requires a high penetration threshold of 75-90%. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel asymmetric communication framework that allows even non-connected vehicles to receive warnings. Experimental results demonstrate that this paradigm achieves better risk mitigation performance. We found that our approach at 25% penetration outperforms the traditional symmetric model at 75%, and benefits saturate at only 50% penetration. This work provides a crucial risk assessment metric and a cost-effective, strategic roadmap for accelerating the safety benefits of V2X deployment.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Intelligible Human-Robot Interaction: An Active Inference Approach to Occluded Pedestrian Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23109)
*Kai Chen,Yuyao Huang,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于主动推理的框架，通过RBPF估计行人混合状态，引入条件信念重置和假设注入技术建模行人潜在意图，使用CEM增强的MPPI控制器进行规划，显著降低遮挡行人场景的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡行人的突然出现是自动驾驶中的关键安全挑战，传统基于规则或纯数据驱动方法难以处理这种长尾场景的高不确定性。

Method: 1. 基于主动推理的框架，赋予智能体类人的信念驱动机制；2. 使用Rao-Blackwellized粒子滤波器高效估计行人的混合状态；3. 引入条件信念重置机制和假设注入技术，显式建模行人多个潜在意图的信念；4. 通过交叉熵方法增强的模型预测路径积分控制器进行规划，结合CEM的高效迭代搜索和MPPI的固有鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真实验表明，相比反应式、基于规则和强化学习基线方法，该方法显著降低了碰撞率，同时展现出可解释的类人驾驶行为，反映了智能体的内部信念状态。

Conclusion: 提出的主动推理框架通过信念驱动的机制有效处理遮挡行人场景的高不确定性，实现了更安全、可解释且类人的自动驾驶行为。

Abstract: The sudden appearance of occluded pedestrians presents a critical safety challenge in autonomous driving. Conventional rule-based or purely data-driven approaches struggle with the inherent high uncertainty of these long-tail scenarios. To tackle this challenge, we propose a novel framework grounded in Active Inference, which endows the agent with a human-like, belief-driven mechanism. Our framework leverages a Rao-Blackwellized Particle Filter (RBPF) to efficiently estimate the pedestrian's hybrid state. To emulate human-like cognitive processes under uncertainty, we introduce a Conditional Belief Reset mechanism and a Hypothesis Injection technique to explicitly model beliefs about the pedestrian's multiple latent intentions. Planning is achieved via a Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) enhanced Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller, which synergizes the efficient, iterative search of CEM with the inherent robustness of MPPI. Simulation experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces the collision rate compared to reactive, rule-based, and reinforcement learning (RL) baselines, while also exhibiting explainable and human-like driving behavior that reflects the agent's internal belief state.

</details>


### [123] [Grasp, Slide, Roll: Comparative Analysis of Contact Modes for Tactile-Based Shape Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23206)
*Chung Hee Kim,Shivani Kamtikar,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir,Joshua Migdal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了三种触觉接触模式（抓取释放、手指滑动、手掌滚动）对物体形状重建的影响，结合信息论探索框架，发现滑动和滚动模式能减少34%的物理交互次数并提高55%的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感能让机器人通过物理交互获取物体的详细几何信息，补充视觉方法。但有效获取有用的触觉数据仍具挑战性，因为物理接触耗时且需要策略性地选择能最大化信息增益同时最小化物理交互的接触位置。

Method: 研究比较了三种接触交互模式：抓取释放、手指滑动诱导的滑动、手掌滚动。这些接触模式与信息论探索框架结合，使用形状补全模型指导后续采样位置。实验使用UR5e机器人手臂配备Inspire-Robots灵巧手进行验证。

Result: 结果显示，手指滑动和手掌滚动模式提高了触觉传感效率，使形状重建收敛更快，减少了34%的物理交互次数，同时将重建精度提高了55%。方法在基本物体几何形状上表现出稳健性能。

Conclusion: 滑动和滚动接触模式比简单的抓取释放更有效地获取触觉信息，结合信息论探索框架能显著提高物体形状重建的效率和精度，为机器人触觉感知提供了优化策略。

Abstract: Tactile sensing allows robots to gather detailed geometric information about objects through physical interaction, complementing vision-based approaches. However, efficiently acquiring useful tactile data remains challenging due to the time-consuming nature of physical contact and the need to strategically choose contact locations that maximize information gain while minimizing physical interactions. This paper studies how different contact modes affect object shape reconstruction using a tactile-enabled dexterous gripper. We compare three contact interaction modes: grasp-releasing, sliding induced by finger-grazing, and palm-rolling. These contact modes are combined with an information-theoretic exploration framework that guides subsequent sampling locations using a shape completion model. Our results show that the improved tactile sensing efficiency of finger-grazing and palm-rolling translates into faster convergence in shape reconstruction, requiring 34% fewer physical interactions while improving reconstruction accuracy by 55%. We validate our approach using a UR5e robot arm equipped with an Inspire-Robots Dexterous Hand, showing robust performance across primitive object geometries.

</details>


### [124] [SPARR: Simulation-based Policies with Asymmetric Real-world Residuals for Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23253)
*Yijie Guo,Iretiayo Akinola,Lars Johannsmeier,Hugo Hadfield,Abhishek Gupta,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARR提出了一种混合方法，将仿真训练的基础策略与真实世界残差策略相结合，以高效适应真实世界变化，在无需人类监督的情况下显著提升装配任务的成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配面临仿真到真实世界的性能退化问题，而纯真实世界强化学习方法又依赖大量人类监督且缺乏泛化能力。需要一种既能利用仿真训练优势，又能适应真实世界变化且无需人类监督的方法。

Method: 提出SPARR混合方法：1）在仿真中训练基础策略，使用低层状态观测和密集奖励；2）在真实世界中学习残差策略，使用视觉观测和稀疏奖励来补偿动力学差异和传感器噪声。基础策略提供初始行为先验，残差策略适应真实世界变化。

Result: 在多样化的两部件装配任务中实现接近完美的成功率。相比最先进的零样本仿真到真实方法，成功率提升38.4%，周期时间减少29.7%。相比依赖人类监督的真实世界RL方法，SPARR无需人类专业知识。

Conclusion: SPARR成功结合了仿真训练和真实世界适应的优势，既避免了仿真到真实世界的性能退化，又无需人类监督，为机器人装配任务提供了一种高效、自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic assembly presents a long-standing challenge due to its requirement for precise, contact-rich manipulation. While simulation-based learning has enabled the development of robust assembly policies, their performance often degrades when deployed in real-world settings due to the sim-to-real gap. Conversely, real-world reinforcement learning (RL) methods avoid the sim-to-real gap, but rely heavily on human supervision and lack generalization ability to environmental changes. In this work, we propose a hybrid approach that combines a simulation-trained base policy with a real-world residual policy to efficiently adapt to real-world variations. The base policy, trained in simulation using low-level state observations and dense rewards, provides strong priors for initial behavior. The residual policy, learned in the real world using visual observations and sparse rewards, compensates for discrepancies in dynamics and sensor noise. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that our method, SPARR, achieves near-perfect success rates across diverse two-part assembly tasks. Compared to the state-of-the-art zero-shot sim-to-real methods, SPARR improves success rates by 38.4% while reducing cycle time by 29.7%. Moreover, SPARR requires no human expertise, in contrast to the state-of-the-art real-world RL approaches that depend heavily on human supervision.

</details>


### [125] [Simple Models, Real Swimming: Digital Twins for Tendon-Driven Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23283)
*Mike Y. Michelis,Nana Obayashi,Josie Hughes,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于简化无状态流体模型的肌腱驱动仿生鱼机器人仿真环境，该模型仅需少量真实游泳轨迹即可匹配实验行为，计算效率高，适用于强化学习等下游控制算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿游泳动物的优雅运动是软体机器人领域的核心挑战，现有建模方法计算成本高，难以用于复杂控制或强化学习。需要一种高效实用的仿真环境来支持水下机器人的学习与控制。

Method: 在MuJoCo机器人框架中构建肌腱驱动仿生鱼机器人模型，采用简化的无状态流体动力学公式。仅使用两条真实游泳轨迹识别五个流体参数，使仿真行为与实验匹配并能在不同驱动频率下泛化。

Result: 该无状态流体模型能够泛化到未见过的驱动模式，性能优于经典的细长体理论等解析模型。仿真速度快于实时，在目标跟踪任务中通过强化学习达到93%的成功率。

Conclusion: 即使简单的无状态模型，只要仔细匹配物理数据，也能作为软体水下机器人的有效数字孪生体，为水生环境中的可扩展学习和控制开辟新方向。

Abstract: Mimicking the graceful motion of swimming animals remains a core challenge in soft robotics due to the complexity of fluid-structure interaction and the difficulty of controlling soft, biomimetic bodies. Existing modeling approaches are often computationally expensive and impractical for complex control or reinforcement learning needed for realistic motions to emerge in robotic systems. In this work, we present a tendon-driven fish robot modeled in an efficient underwater swimmer environment using a simplified, stateless hydrodynamics formulation implemented in the widespread robotics framework MuJoCo. With just two real-world swimming trajectories, we identify five fluid parameters that allow a matching to experimental behavior and generalize across a range of actuation frequencies. We show that this stateless fluid model can generalize to unseen actuation and outperform classical analytical models such as the elongated body theory. This simulation environment runs faster than real-time and can easily enable downstream learning algorithms such as reinforcement learning for target tracking, reaching a 93% success rate. Due to the simplicity and ease of use of the model and our open-source simulation environment, our results show that even simple, stateless models -- when carefully matched to physical data -- can serve as effective digital twins for soft underwater robots, opening up new directions for scalable learning and control in aquatic environments.

</details>


### [126] [Interface-Aware Trajectory Reconstruction of Limited Demonstrations for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23287)
*Demiana R. Barsoum,Mahdieh Nejati Javaremi,Larisa Y. C. Loke,Brenna D. Argall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轨迹重建算法，通过推理任务、环境和接口约束，将低维接口演示提升到机器人完整控制空间，解决接口限制导致的次优运动问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助机器人用户通常通过低维接口（如1维吸吹接口）控制高自由度机器人，这种维度不匹配导致只能访问部分控制维度，使演示嵌入反映接口限制而非用户意图的次优运动

Method: 开发轨迹重建算法，推理任务约束、环境约束和接口约束，将接口受限的演示提升到机器人的完整控制空间

Result: 使用2维操纵杆和1维吸吹接口控制两个不同的7自由度机械臂进行ADL任务演示评估，重建后的轨迹比接口受限的对应轨迹更快、更高效，同时尊重用户偏好

Conclusion: 轨迹重建算法能够有效解决接口维度不匹配问题，将低维接口演示提升到完整控制空间，生成更优的运动轨迹，同时保持用户意图和偏好

Abstract: Assistive robots offer agency to humans with severe motor impairments. Often, these users control high-DoF robots through low-dimensional interfaces, such as using a 1-D sip-and-puff interface to operate a 6-DoF robotic arm. This mismatch results in having access to only a subset of control dimensions at a given time, imposing unintended and artificial constraints on robot motion. As a result, interface-limited demonstrations embed suboptimal motions that reflect interface restrictions rather than user intent. To address this, we present a trajectory reconstruction algorithm that reasons about task, environment, and interface constraints to lift demonstrations into the robot's full control space. We evaluate our approach using real-world demonstrations of ADL-inspired tasks performed via a 2-D joystick and 1-D sip-and-puff control interface, teleoperating two distinct 7-DoF robotic arms. Analyses of the reconstructed demonstrations and derived control policies show that lifted trajectories are faster and more efficient than their interface-constrained counterparts while respecting user preferences.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Conversational Successes and Breakdowns in Everyday Non-Display Smart Glasses Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22340)
*Xiuqi Tommy Zhu,Xiaoan Liu,Casper Harteveld,Smit Desai,Eileen McGivney*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过为期一个月的协作式自我民族志研究，分析了无显示屏智能眼镜在日常使用中的对话成功与失败模式，并与先前语音交互研究对比，揭示了此类设备的独特优势和设计机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无显示屏智能眼镜结合环境感知和纯语音交互（基于大语言模型）具有支持日常活动的潜力。了解日常情境中对话成功与失败的模式，可以为未来纯语音界面的设计提供更好的指导。

Method: 采用为期一个月的协作式自我民族志方法（n=2），识别使用无显示屏智能眼镜时的成功与失败模式，并将这些模式与先前关于纯语音交互的研究发现进行比较。

Result: 识别了无显示屏智能眼镜在日常使用中的具体成功与失败模式，通过与先前语音交互研究的比较，突出了此类设备提供的独特功能和机会。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了无显示屏智能眼镜在日常语音交互中的独特模式，为未来纯语音界面的设计提供了重要见解，强调了环境感知与语音交互结合的特殊优势。

Abstract: Non-Display Smart Glasses hold the potential to support everyday activities by combining continuous environmental sensing with voice-only interaction powered by large language models (LLMs). Understanding how conversational successes and breakdowns arise in everyday contexts can better inform the design of future voice-only interfaces. To investigate this, we conducted a month-long collaborative autoethnography (n=2) to identify patterns of successes and breakdowns when using such devices. We then compare these patterns with prior findings on voice-only interactions to highlight the unique affordances and opportunities offered by non-display smart glasses.

</details>


### [128] [VoiceAlign: A Shimming Layer for Enhancing the Usability of Legacy Voice User Interface Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22374)
*Md Ehtesham-Ul-Haque,Syed Masum Billah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VoiceAlign是一个自适应中间层，利用大语言模型将自然语音命令转换为符合传统VUI系统要求的格式，无需修改现有系统即可显著提升语音交互效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管商业VUI系统具备强大的技术能力，但由于固定命令格式需要精确措辞、限制性超时机制在规划暂停时丢弃输入、以及反馈不足阻碍多步骤交互等问题，导致操作系统内置语音命令使用率低下。

Method: 开发VoiceAlign自适应中间层，通过大语言模型拦截自然语音命令并将其转换为符合要求的语法格式，通过虚拟音频通道透明传输给底层系统。基于研究创建合成数据集，微调小型语言模型实现本地服务。

Result: VoiceAlign将命令失败率降低一半，每个任务所需命令减少25%，显著降低认知和时间需求。微调的小型语言模型在本地服务时达到90%以上准确率，响应时间200毫秒，无需依赖第三方API。

Conclusion: 现代AI技术可以在不修改系统的情况下释放传统VUI系统的未充分利用潜力，为现有基础设施提供实用解决方案，实现边缘设备的实时交互。

Abstract: Voice user interfaces (VUIs) are rapidly transitioning from accessibility features to mainstream interaction modalities. Yet most operating systems' built-in voice commands remain underutilized despite possessing robust technical capabilities. Through our analysis of four commercial VUI systems and a formative study with 16 participants, we found that fixed command formats require exact phrasing, restrictive timeout mechanisms discard input during planning pauses, and insufficient feedback hampers multi-step interactions. To address these challenges, we developed VoiceAlign, an adaptive shimming layer that mediates between users and legacy VUI systems. VoiceAlign intercepts natural voice commands, transforms them to match the required syntax using a large language model, and transmits these adapted commands through a virtual audio channel that remains transparent to the underlying system. In our evaluation with 12 participants, VoiceAlign reduced command failures by half, required 25% fewer commands per task, and significantly lowered cognitive and temporal demands when paired with an existing legacy VUI system. Furthermore, we created a synthetic dataset informed by our studies and fine-tuned a small language model that achieves over 90% accuracy with 200 ms response time when served locally, eliminating dependence on third-party APIs while enabling real-time interaction on edge devices. This work demonstrates how modern AI techniques can unlock the underutilized potential of legacy VUI systems without requiring system modifications, offering a practical solution without replacing existing infrastructure.

</details>


### [129] [The Way We Notice, That's What Really Matters: Instantiating UI Components with Distinguishing Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22436)
*Priyan Vaithilingam,Alan Leung,Jeffrey Nichols,Titus Barik*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出"区分性变体"概念，结合符号推理和LLM生成既逼真又独特的UI组件实例，开发Celestial工具帮助前端开发者探索组件设计空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前端开发者创建可复用的UI组件时，需要参数化视觉和行为属性，这使得实例化变得困难，开发者必须处理大量属性值及其交互，并探索组件的庞大设计空间以提供逼真自然的属性值

Method: 将区分性变体生成框架化为设计空间采样，结合符号推理识别视觉重要属性，使用LLM驱动的逼真采样器从其世界知识中生成逼真实例，并实现为Celestial工具

Result: 12名前端开发者参与的研究表明，参与者认为这些变体对比较和映射组件设计空间有用，逼真实例具有领域相关性，Celestial将组件实例化从手动过程转变为结构化探索活动

Conclusion: 区分性变体通过结合符号推理和LLM知识，有效解决了UI组件实例化难题，Celestial工具成功将组件实例化转变为结构化探索过程，提高了前端开发效率

Abstract: Front-end developers author UI components to be broadly reusable by parameterizing visual and behavioral properties. While flexible, this makes instantiation harder, as developers must reason about numerous property values and interactions. In practice, they must explore the component's large design space and provide realistic and natural values to properties. To address this, we introduce distinguishing variations: variations that are both mimetic and distinct. We frame distinguishing variation generation as design-space sampling, combining symbolic inference to identify visually important properties with an LLM-driven mimetic sampler to produce realistic instantiations from its world knowledge. We instantiate distinguishing variations in Celestial, a tool that helps developers explore and visualize distinguishing variations. In a study with front-end developers (n=12), participants found these variations useful for comparing and mapping component design spaces, reported that mimetic instantiations were domain-relevant, and validated that Celestial transformed component instantiation from a manual process into a structured, exploratory activity.

</details>


### [130] [Evaluating Zero-Shot and One-Shot Adaptation of Small Language Models in Leader-Follower Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23312)
*Rafael R. Baptista,André de Lima Salgado,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker,Thiago Boaventura,Gustavo J. G. Lahr*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了小型语言模型在人类-机器人交互中领导者-跟随者角色分类的性能，发现微调的Qwen2.5-0.5B模型在零样本模式下达到86.66%准确率，延迟仅22.2ms，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的移动和辅助机器人上实时分配领导者-跟随者角色具有挑战性。大型语言模型虽然自然沟通能力强，但尺寸和延迟限制了其在设备上的部署。小型语言模型作为潜在替代方案，其在HRI角色分类中的有效性尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 提出了一个用于领导者-跟随者通信的SLM基准测试，引入了基于已发布数据库并增强合成样本的新数据集以捕捉交互特定动态。研究了两种适应策略：提示工程和微调，在零样本和单样本交互模式下进行比较，并与未训练基线对比。使用Qwen2.5-0.5B模型进行实验。

Result: 零样本微调实现了稳健的分类性能（86.66%准确率），同时保持低延迟（每样本22.2ms），显著优于基线和提示工程方法。但在单样本模式下性能下降，增加的上下文长度挑战了模型的架构容量。

Conclusion: 微调的SLM为直接角色分配提供了有效解决方案，同时凸显了边缘设备上对话复杂性与分类可靠性之间的关键权衡。研究证明了SLM在实时HRI角色分类中的可行性，但需要注意上下文长度对性能的影响。

Abstract: Leader-follower interaction is an important paradigm in human-robot interaction (HRI). Yet, assigning roles in real time remains challenging for resource-constrained mobile and assistive robots. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for natural communication, their size and latency limit on-device deployment. Small language models (SLMs) offer a potential alternative, but their effectiveness for role classification in HRI has not been systematically evaluated. In this paper, we present a benchmark of SLMs for leader-follower communication, introducing a novel dataset derived from a published database and augmented with synthetic samples to capture interaction-specific dynamics. We investigate two adaptation strategies: prompt engineering and fine-tuning, studied under zero-shot and one-shot interaction modes, compared with an untrained baseline. Experiments with Qwen2.5-0.5B reveal that zero-shot fine-tuning achieves robust classification performance (86.66% accuracy) while maintaining low latency (22.2 ms per sample), significantly outperforming baseline and prompt-engineered approaches. However, results also indicate a performance degradation in one-shot modes, where increased context length challenges the model's architectural capacity. These findings demonstrate that fine-tuned SLMs provide an effective solution for direct role assignment, while highlighting critical trade-offs between dialogue complexity and classification reliability on the edge.

</details>


### [131] [Skewed Dual Normal Distribution Model: Predicting 1D Touch Pointing Success Rate for Targets Near Screen Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22454)
*Nobuhito Kasahara,Shota Yamanaka,Homei Miyashita*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出偏斜双正态分布模型，用于预测屏幕边缘附近目标的触摸点击成功率，解决了现有模型忽略边缘目标的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触摸点击成功率预测模型通常排除屏幕边缘附近的目标，但实际UI设计中经常需要将目标放置在边缘附近，且可滚动UI中任何元素都可能移动到边缘位置。需要建立模型来量化目标-边缘距离对一维触摸指向精度的影响。

Method: 提出偏斜双正态分布模型，假设点击坐标分布受到附近边缘的影响而发生偏斜。通过两个智能手机实验验证模型，测量目标接近边缘时点击分布的变化。

Result: 实验结果显示，随着目标接近边缘，点击分布的峰值向边缘移动，尾部向远离边缘的方向延伸。与先前研究相反，当目标接触边缘时成功率反而提高，表明存在"将目标与边缘一起点击"的策略。模型通过考虑偏斜效应，能够准确预测包括边缘相邻目标在内的各种条件下的成功率。

Conclusion: 偏斜双正态分布模型扩展了触摸点击成功率预测的覆盖范围至整个屏幕，包括边缘区域，为UI设计支持工具提供了更全面的指导。

Abstract: Typical success-rate prediction models for tapping exclude targets near screen edges; however, design constraints often force such placements. Additionally, in scrollable UIs any element can move close to an edge. In this work, we model how target--edge distance affects 1D touch pointing accuracy. We propose the Skewed Dual Normal Distribution Model, which assumes the tap coordinate distribution is skewed by a nearby edge. The results of two smartphone experiments showed that, as targets approached the edge, the distribution's peak shifted toward the edge and its tail extended away. In contrast to prior reports, the success rate improved when the target touched the edge, suggesting a strategy of ``tapping the target together with the edge.'' By accounting for skew, our model predicts success rates across a wide range of conditions, including edge-adjacent targets, thus extending coverage to the whole screen and informing UI design support tools.

</details>


### [132] [Relational Appliances: A Robot in the Refrigerator for Home-Based Health Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22542)
*Timothy Bickmore,Mehdi Arjmand,Yunus Terzioglu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 探索"关系型家电"概念，通过在冰箱中部署拟人化机器人头部来促进健康零食选择，研究发现机器人具有说服力且社交参与度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厨房家电位于日常饮食决策的关键位置，是健康促进的潜在但未充分探索的场所。研究者希望探索关系型家电的概念，即设计为具身社会行动者的日常家用设备，通过持续个性化互动与用户互动。

Method: 研究聚焦冰箱，利用其固定、传感器丰富的环境、私人互动空间以及与食品的紧密联系等独特特性。通过试点研究，在家庭实验室公寓中部署了冰箱内的拟人化机器人头部。参与者在模拟电视"广告时间"期间反复取零食，同时与真人大小机器人头部互动。参与者被随机分配到健康促进条件（机器人提供健康零食建议）或社交聊天控制条件。测量结果包括建议依从性、所选零食的营养质量以及与机器人接受度相关的心理社会指标。

Result: 参与者认为机器人具有说服力、社交参与度高，且随时间推移越来越自然，常将其描述为有帮助、有意识和伴侣式的。大多数参与者报告对零食决策有更高意识，并表示有兴趣在自己家中拥有这样的机器人。

Conclusion: 研究讨论了设计关系型家电的启示，这些家电利用拟人化、信任和长期人机关系来实现家庭健康促进。冰箱作为关系型家电具有独特潜力，能够通过情境化、对话式互动影响饮食决策。

Abstract: Kitchen appliances are frequently used domestic artifacts situated at the point of everyday dietary decision making, making them a promising but underexplored site for health promotion. We explore the concept of relational appliances: everyday household devices designed as embodied social actors that engage users through ongoing, personalized interaction. We focus on the refrigerator, whose unique affordances, including a fixed, sensor-rich environment, private interaction space, and close coupling to food items, support contextualized, conversational engagement during snack choices. We present an initial exploration of this concept through a pilot study deploying an anthropomorphic robotic head inside a household refrigerator. In a home-lab apartment, participants repeatedly retrieved snacks during simulated TV "commercial breaks" while interacting with a human-sized robotic head. Participants were randomized to either a health-promotion condition, in which the robot made healthy snack recommendations, or a social-chat control condition. Outcomes included compliance with recommendations, nutritional quality of selected snacks, and psychosocial measures related to acceptance of the robot. Results suggest that participants found the robot persuasive, socially engaging, and increasingly natural over time, often describing it as helpful, aware, and companionable. Most participants reported greater awareness of their snack decisions and expressed interest in having such a robot in their own home. We discuss implications for designing relational appliances that leverage anthropomorphism, trust, and long-term human-technology relationships for home-based health promotion.

</details>


### [133] [DuoMorph: Synergistic Integration of FDM Printing and Pneumatic Actuation for Shape-Changing Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22604)
*Xueqing Li,Danqi huang,Tianyu Yu,Shuzi Yin,Bingjie Gao,Anna Matsumoto,Zhihao Yao,Yiwei Zhao,Shiqing Lyu,Yuchen Tian,Lining Yao,Haipeng Mi,Qiuyu Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DuoMorph是一种结合FDM 3D打印和气动驱动的设计制造方法，通过单一FDM打印机实现打印结构与气动元件的协同设计和一体化制造，创建新型变形界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变形界面通常需要复杂的多步骤制造工艺，且打印结构与气动元件难以协同工作。DuoMorph旨在通过单一FDM打印机实现打印结构与气动元件的无缝集成，简化制造流程并增强功能。

Method: 提出四类基本交互类别定义设计空间，开发配套制造方法和设计工具。通过FDM打印与热封工艺结合，在单一设备中同时完成3D/4D打印和气动元件制造，实现打印结构与气动元件的相互驱动和约束。

Result: 成功开发了DuoMorph系统，包括设计工具和制造方法。通过示例应用和性能演示展示了该方法能够实现传统方法难以达到的变形功能，验证了单一FDM打印机完成完整混合结构制造的可行性。

Conclusion: DuoMorph为变形界面设计提供了新的范式，通过打印结构与气动元件的协同设计，在单一制造流程中实现复杂功能，为可变形交互设备开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: We introduce DuoMorph, a design and fabrication method that synergistically integrates Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) printing and pneumatic actuation to create novel shape-changing interfaces. In DuoMorph, the printed structures and heat-sealed pneumatic elements are mutually designed to actuate and constrain each other, enabling functions that are difficult for either component to achieve in isolation. Moreover, the entire hybrid structure can be fabricated through a single, seamless process using only a standard FDM printer, including both heat-sealing and 3D and 4D printing. In this paper, we define a design space including four primitive categories that capture the fundamental ways in which printed and pneumatic components can interact. To support this process, we present a fabrication method and an accompanying design tool. Finally, we demonstrate the potential of DuoMorph through a series of example applications and performance demonstrations.

</details>


### [134] [CoLyricist: Enhancing Lyric Writing with AI through Workflow-Aligned Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22606)
*Masahiro Yoshida,Bingxuan Li,Songyan Zhao,Qinyi Zhou,Shiwei Hu,Xiang Anthony Chen,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CoLyricist是一款AI辅助歌词创作工具，通过理解专业作词人的工作流程，提供针对性的AI支持，提升创作效率，适用于新手和经验丰富的作词人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有歌词创作工具未能充分理解作词人的工作阶段和挑战，导致设计效果不佳。作词人有独特的工作流程，但许多遵循共同阶段，工具需要适应这些阶段才能有效融入创作实践。

Method: 通过半结构化访谈10位经验丰富的作词人进行形成性研究，识别出四个关键阶段：主题设定、构思、歌词草拟和旋律适配。CoLyricist为每个阶段提供定制的AI驱动支持。随后对16名参与者（包括经验丰富和新手作词人）进行用户研究，评估工具效果。

Result: CoLyricist提升了不同技能水平用户的歌曲创作体验。新手用户特别欣赏旋律适配功能，而经验丰富的用户则重视构思支持。

Conclusion: 通过理解作词人的工作流程并针对每个阶段提供AI支持，CoLyricist能够有效提升创作效率，适用于不同经验水平的作词人，证明了工作流程对齐设计的重要性。

Abstract: We propose CoLyricist, an AI-assisted lyric writing tool designed to support the typical workflows of experienced lyricists and enhance their creative efficiency. While lyricists have unique processes, many follow common stages. Tools that fail to accommodate these stages challenge integration into creative practices. Existing research and tools lack sufficient understanding of these songwriting stages and their associated challenges, resulting in ineffective designs. Through a formative study involving semi-structured interviews with 10 experienced lyricists, we identified four key stages: Theme Setting, Ideation, Drafting Lyrics, and Melody Fitting. CoLyricist addresses these needs by incorporating tailored AI-driven support for each stage, optimizing the lyric writing process to be more seamless and efficient. To examine whether this workflow-aligned design also benefits those without prior experience, we conducted a user study with 16 participants, including both experienced and novice lyricists. Results showed that CoLyricist enhances the songwriting experience across skill levels. Novice users especially appreciated the Melody-Fitting feature, while experienced users valued the Ideation support.

</details>


### [135] [Simulation-based Optimization for Augmented Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22735)
*Yunpeng Bai,Shengdong Zhao,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 将增强阅读系统框架化为基于资源理性阅读模型的仿真优化问题，提出离线探索和在线个性化两条优化管道


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增强阅读系统依赖启发式方法、不透明的数据驱动模型或重复的人工设计循环，缺乏系统性和可解释性

Method: 将增强阅读构建为基于资源理性人类阅读模型的仿真优化问题，提出离线设计探索和在线实时个性化两条互补的优化管道

Result: 该框架能够实现自适应、可解释、可扩展的增强阅读设计，无需完全依赖人工测试

Conclusion: 基于仿真的优化方法为增强阅读系统设计提供了系统化、可解释且可扩展的新途径

Abstract: Augmented reading systems aim to adapt text presentation to improve comprehension and task performance, yet existing approaches rely heavily on heuristics, opaque data-driven models, or repeated human involvement in the design loop. We propose framing augmented reading as a simulation-based optimization problem grounded in resource-rational models of human reading. These models instantiate a simulated reader that allocates limited cognitive resources, such as attention, memory, and time under task demands, enabling systematic evaluation of text user interfaces. We introduce two complementary optimization pipelines: an offline approach that explores design alternatives using simulated readers, and an online approach that personalizes reading interfaces in real time using ongoing interaction data. Together, this perspective enables adaptive, explainable, and scalable augmented reading design without relying solely on human testing.

</details>


### [136] [An AI-Based Structured Semantic Control Model for Stable and Coherent Dynamic Interactive Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22762)
*Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出可控生成框架解决生成模型在复杂交互场景中平衡灵活性、稳定性和可控性的挑战，通过结构化语义状态空间和多级约束实现动态交互内容的可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在复杂交互场景中难以平衡灵活性、稳定性和可控性，需要一种能够有效控制动态交互内容生成的方法。

Method: 构建结构化语义状态空间编码用户输入、环境条件和历史上下文为可操作的潜在表示，生成方向控制向量指导内容生成，引入语义一致性约束、结构稳定性约束和语义漂移惩罚等多级约束。

Result: 在公开对话数据集上的敏感性分析表明，该方法改善了语义结构、上下文一致性和可控表达，为交互内容生成提供了结构化有效解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的可控生成框架通过结构化语义建模和多级约束机制，有效解决了生成模型在动态交互场景中的平衡问题，提升了内容生成的质量和可控性。

Abstract: This study addresses the challenge that generative models struggle to balance flexibility, stability, and controllability in complex interactive scenarios. It proposes a controllable generation framework for dynamic interactive content construction. The framework builds a structured semantic state space that encodes user input, environmental conditions, and historical context into actionable latent representations and generates directional control vectors to guide the content generation process. It introduces multilevel constraints, including semantic consistency constraints, structural stability constraints, and semantic drift penalties, which help the model maintain clear semantic paths and coherent logic in dynamic environments. These constraints prevent content deviation, unstable tone, or structural breaks. Based on these components, the study designs a systematic controllable generation pipeline in which semantic modeling, control signals, and generation strategies work together within one framework. Sensitivity analyses on control vector dimension, hidden layer size, noise intensity, and training sample scale are conducted on a public dialogue dataset to validate the framework. The results show that the approach improves semantic structure, contextual consistency, and controllable expression, providing a structured and effective solution for interactive content generation.

</details>


### [137] [TherapyProbe: Generating Design Knowledge for Relational Safety in Mental Health Chatbots Through Adversarial Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22775)
*Joydeep Chandra,Satyam Kumar Navneet,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TherapyProbe：一种通过对抗性多智能体模拟评估心理健康聊天机器人关系安全性的设计探测方法，识别跨对话的交互模式失败并转化为设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康聊天机器人安全评估仅关注单轮危机响应，忽略了跨对话展开的交互模式质量（关系安全性），无法评估聊天机器人随时间推移是帮助还是伤害用户。

Method: 提出TherapyProbe设计探测方法，使用开源模型通过对抗性多智能体模拟系统探索聊天机器人对话轨迹，识别关系安全失败模式。

Result: 识别出"验证螺旋"（聊天机器人逐步强化绝望感）和"共情疲劳"（响应变得机械化）等交互模式失败，转化为包含23个失败原型的"安全模式库"及相应设计建议。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：1）无需API成本的可复制方法；2）临床基础失败分类法；3）为开发者、临床医生和政策制定者提供设计启示，推动心理健康聊天机器人关系安全设计。

Abstract: As mental health chatbots proliferate to address the global treatment gap, a critical question emerges: How do we design for relational safety the quality of interaction patterns that unfold across conversations rather than the correctness of individual responses? Current safety evaluations assess single-turn crisis responses, missing the therapeutic dynamics that determine whether chatbots help or harm over time. We introduce TherapyProbe, a design probe methodology that generates actionable design knowledge by systematically exploring chatbot conversation trajectories through adversarial multi-agent simulation. Using open-source models, TherapyProbe surfaces relational safety failures interaction patterns like "validation spirals" where chatbots progressively reinforce hopelessness, or "empathy fatigue" where responses become mechanical over turns. Our contribution is translating these failures into a Safety Pattern Library of 23 failure archetypes with corresponding design recommendations. We contribute: (1) a replicable methodology requiring no API costs, (2) a clinically-grounded failure taxonomy, and (3) design implications for developers, clinicians, and policymakers.

</details>


### [138] [Input-Envelope-Output: Auditable Generative Music Rewards in Sensory-Sensitive Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22813)
*Cong Ye,Songlin Shang,Xiaoxu Ma,Xiangbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出I-E-O框架，在感官敏感环境中通过显式安全边界层解决生成反馈的安全与参与度平衡问题，并以MusiBubbles原型验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感官敏感环境（如自闭症谱系障碍）中的生成反馈设计面临核心挑战：个体感官耐受度差异大，难以在保证安全的同时维持参与度。现有交互音乐系统通常将安全性隐式编码在直接输入-输出映射中，这虽能保持新颖性但使系统行为难以预测或审计。

Method: 提出约束优先的输入-包络-输出（I-E-O）框架，在用户输入和音频输出之间引入低风险包络层，明确指定安全边界、确定性执行边界并记录干预以供审计。从该架构推导出四个可验证设计原则，并在基于Web的原型MusiBubbles中实例化。

Result: 开发了I-E-O架构、MusiBubbles作为示例实现，以及支持在自闭症谱系障碍和其他感官敏感领域采用的可复现性包。

Conclusion: I-E-O框架通过显式安全边界层解决了感官敏感环境中生成反馈的安全与参与度平衡问题，使系统行为可预测、可审计，同时保持动作-输出因果关系，为自闭症谱系障碍等感官敏感领域提供了可验证的设计方法。

Abstract: Generative feedback in sensory-sensitive contexts poses a core design challenge: large individual differences in sensory tolerance make it difficult to sustain engagement without compromising safety. This tension is exemplified in autism spectrum disorder (ASD), where auditory sensitivities are common yet highly heterogeneous. Existing interactive music systems typically encode safety implicitly within direct input-output (I-O) mappings, which can preserve novelty but make system behavior hard to predict or audit. We instead propose a constraint-first Input-Envelope-Output (I-E-O) framework that makes safety explicit and verifiable while preserving action-output causality. I-E-O introduces a low-risk envelope layer between user input and audio output to specify safe bounds, enforce them deterministically, and log interventions for audit. From this architecture, we derive four verifiable design principles and instantiate them in MusiBubbles, a web-based prototype. Contributions include the I-E-O architecture, MusiBubbles as an exemplar implementation, and a reproducibility package to support adoption in ASD and other sensory-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [139] [They Think AI Can Do More Than It Actually Can: Practices, Challenges, & Opportunities of AI-Supported Reporting In Local Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22887)
*Besjon Cifliku,Hendrik Heuer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究德国地方新闻记者如何运用数据和AI进行报道，分析其面临的挑战，并提出基于记者社会技术视角的AI支持报道改进建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地方报纸收入下降促使新闻编辑室采用自动化技术以保持效率并持续为社区提供信息。然而，当前研究对地方记者如何运用数字数据以及哪些新闻编辑流程最能受益于AI支持的数据报道缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对德国地方新闻记者进行了21次半结构化访谈，从话语设计视角探讨AI支持报道系统的自我感知机会。

Result: 研究发现地方记者未能充分利用AI支持数据相关工作的潜力。尽管地方记者对AI能力认知有限，但他们愿意使用AI处理数据和发现新闻故事。研究还揭示了记者在与数据和AI互动时面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 基于记者的社会技术视角和他们想象的AI未来能力，提出了改进地方新闻中AI支持报道的具体建议，为地方新闻编辑室更有效地整合AI技术提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Declining newspaper revenues prompt local newsrooms to adopt automation to maintain efficiency and keep the community informed. However, current research provides a limited understanding of how local journalists work with digital data and which newsroom processes would benefit most from AI-supported (data) reporting. To bridge this gap, we conducted 21 semi-structured interviews with local journalists in Germany. Our study investigates how local journalists use data and AI (RQ1); the challenges they encounter when interacting with data and AI (RQ2); and the self-perceived opportunities of AI-supported reporting systems through the lens of discursive design (RQ3). Our findings reveal that local journalists do not fully leverage AI's potential to support data-related work. Despite local journalists' limited awareness of AI's capabilities, they are willing to use it to process data and discover stories. Finally, we provide recommendations for improving AI-supported reporting in the context of local news, grounded in the journalists' socio-technical perspective and their imagined AI future capabilities.

</details>


### [140] [InfoAlign: A Human-AI Co-Creation System for Storytelling with Infographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22901)
*Jielin Feng,Xinwu Ye,Qianhui Li,Verena Ingrid Prantl,Jun-Hsiang Yao,Yuheng Zhao,Yun Wang,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: InfoAlign是一个人类-AI协同创作系统，通过叙事中心的工作流程将长文本转换为故事驱动的信息图表，保持故事一致性并支持用户意图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息图表创作工具缺乏对故事一致性的支持，无法在整个设计过程中保持与用户故事目标的对齐，需要解决这一设计空白

Method: 通过形成性访谈和定量分析识别设计需求和常见故事布局模式，提出包含故事构建、视觉编码和空间构成的三阶段叙事中心工作流程，开发InfoAlign系统实现人类-AI协同创作

Result: InfoAlign能够在创作阶段保持故事连贯性，有效支持人类-AI协同创作故事驱动信息图表设计，用户可在任何阶段介入和优化设计

Conclusion: InfoAlign通过叙事中心的工作流程和人类-AI协同创作方法，成功解决了信息图表创作中故事一致性和用户意图保持的问题

Abstract: Storytelling infographics are a powerful medium for communicating data-driven stories through visual presentation. However, existing authoring tools lack support for maintaining story consistency and aligning with users' story goals throughout the design process. To address this gap, we conducted formative interviews and a quantitative analysis to identify design needs and common story-informed layout patterns in infographics. Based on these insights, we propose a narrative-centric workflow for infographic creation consisting of three phases: story construction, visual encoding, and spatial composition. Building on this workflow, we developed InfoAlign, a human-AI co-creation system that transforms long or unstructured text into stories, recommends semantically aligned visual designs, and generates layout blueprints. Users can intervene and refine the design at any stage, ensuring their intent is preserved and the infographic creation process remains transparent. Evaluations show that InfoAlign preserves story coherence across authoring stages and effectively supports human-AI co-creation for storytelling infographic design.

</details>


### [141] [TableTale: Reviving the Narrative Interplay Between Data Tables and Text in Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22908)
*Liangwei Wang,Zhengxuan Zhang,Yifan Cao,Fugee Tsung,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TableTale：一个增强的阅读界面，通过多粒度文本-表格联动和渐进式视觉提示，降低科学论文中数据表格的认知负荷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中的数据表格通常需要与周围文本共同解释，这种叙事互动给读者带来认知负担，现有界面未能充分支持这种阅读过程

Method: 通过形成性研究分析文本-表格叙事互动特征，开发TableTale界面，实现多粒度文本增强（段落、句子、提及）、自动构建文档级链接模式，以及渐进式渲染级联视觉提示

Result: 24名参与者的受试者内研究表明，TableTale显著降低了认知负荷并提高了阅读效率

Conclusion: TableTale展示了增强论文阅读体验的潜力，为未来阅读界面设计提供了参考

Abstract: Data tables play a central role in scientific papers. However, their meaning is often co-constructed with surrounding text through narrative interplay, making comprehension cognitively demanding for readers. In this work, we explore how interfaces can better support this reading process. We conducted a formative study that revealed key characteristics of text-table narrative interplay, including linking mechanisms, multi-granularity alignments, and mention typologies, as well as a layered framework of readers' intents. Informed by these insights, we present TableTale, an augmented reading interface that enriches text with data tables at multiple granularities, including paragraphs, sentences, and mentions. TableTale automatically constructs a document-level linking schema within the paper and progressively renders cascade visual cues on text and tables that unfold as readers move through the text. A within-subject study with 24 participants showed that TableTale reduced cognitive workload and improved reading efficiency, demonstrating its potential to enhance paper reading and inform future reading interface design.

</details>


### [142] [Understanding Older Adults' Experiences of Support, Concerns, and Risks from Kinship-Role AI-Generated Influencers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22993)
*Tianqi Song,Black Sun,Jingshu Li,Han Li,Chi-Lan Yang,Yijia Xu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了中国短视频平台上AI生成的影响者（特别是扮演孙辈角色的AI）如何通过视觉和对话线索建立亲属角色，满足老年人情感和信息需求，但也引发情感替代和不平等情感投入的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的虚拟影响者在中国短视频平台日益流行，常采用亲属角色（如AI孙辈）吸引老年人。尽管这一趋势引发公众关注，但对其设计策略、老年人体验以及相关利弊缺乏深入了解。

Method: 结合社交媒体分析和访谈方法，研究AI影响者的设计策略和老年人体验。

Result: 研究发现：1）影响者通过视觉和对话线索构建亲属角色，促使观众参与亲属角色扮演；2）访谈显示这些线索能引发情感共鸣，满足老年人的信息和情感需求；3）同时也引发情感替代和不平等情感投入的担忧。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了虚拟形象与真实家庭关系之间的复杂关系，这种关系受更广泛的社会文化规范影响。讨论了AI如何在文化背景下加强老年人的社会支持，同时减轻相关风险。

Abstract: AI-generated influencers are rapidly gaining popularity on Chinese short-video platforms, often adopting kinship-based roles such as AI grandchildren to attract older adults. Although this trend has raised public concern, little is known about the design strategies behind these influencers, how older adults experience them, and the benefits and risks involved. In this study, we combined social media analysis with interviews to unpack the above questions. Our findings show that influencers use both visual and conversational cues to enact kinship roles, prompting audiences to engage in kinship-based role-play. Interviews further show that these cues arouse emotional resonance, help fulfill older adults' informational and emotional needs, while also raising concerns about emotional displacement and unequal emotional investment. We highlight the complex relationship between virtual avatars and real family ties, shaped by broader sociocultural norms, and discuss how AI might strengthen social support for older adults while mitigating risks within cultural contexts.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Faders: Understanding 6DoF Gesture Ecologies in Music Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23090)
*Jeremy Wertheim Co Chen,Rendell Christian Ngo,Cedric Matthew Yu,Hans Emilio Lumagui,Ethan Badayos,Jordan Aiko Deja*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: XR音乐混音中6DoF手势的生态有效性研究：通过设计工作坊和用户研究评估空间手势在增益、压缩、均衡和自动化等核心音频任务中的可用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实(XR)通过超越2D推子实现具身化空间交互，为音乐混音提供了新的工作流程。然而，目前尚不清楚哪些六自由度(6DoF)手势符合实际混音实践，以及此类交互是否能支持可管理的认知负荷和积极的用户体验。

Method: 1. 与经验丰富的混音师进行设计工作坊，为增益、压缩、均衡和自动化等核心音频任务提取手势概念；2. 在XR原型中实现这些手势；3. 开展用户研究(n=12)，使用认知负荷测量、用户体验评分和访谈评估手势的生态有效性。

Result: 参与者普遍认为6DoF手势直观且与混音任务映射良好，报告了强烈的沉浸感和与音频环境的连接感。不同手势间的认知负荷差异很小，但参与者基于工作流程熟悉度和感知控制表达了偏好。

Conclusion: 研究为设计XR混音工具提供了启示，需要在表达性、精确性和生态有效性之间取得平衡，以支持直观且高效的空间交互。

Abstract: Extended reality (XR) enables new music-mixing workflows by moving beyond 2D faders toward embodied, spatial interaction. However, it remains unclear which six-degree-of-freedom (6DoF) gestures align with real-world mixing practices and whether such interactions support manageable cognitive load and positive user experience. We conducted a design workshop with experienced mixers to elicit gesture concepts for core audio tasks gain, compression, equalization, and automation, and implemented these in an XR prototype. A user study (n=12) evaluated the ecological validity of the gestures using cognitive load measures, user-experience ratings, and interviews. Participants generally found 6DoF gestures intuitive and well-mapped to mixing tasks, reporting strong immersion and a sense of connection with the audio environment. Cognitive load differences across gestures were minimal, though participants expressed preferences shaped by workflow familiarity and perceived control. We discuss implications for designing XR mixing tools that balance expressiveness, precision, and ecological validity.

</details>


### [144] [TaleBot: A Tangible AI Companion to Support Children in Co-creative Storytelling for Resilience Cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23095)
*Yonglin Chen,Jingjing Zhang,Kezhuo Wang,Pengcheng An,Xueliang Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TaleBot是一个AI赋能的系统，支持儿童根据个人情境共同创作关于克服日常逆境的故事，旨在帮助儿童建立心理韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韧性是影响儿童心理健康和未来发展的关键因素，但HCI领域在如何通过逆境体验帮助儿童建立韧性方面的研究有限。该研究旨在探索如何利用技术帮助儿童发展心理韧性。

Method: 首先对小学教师和专业心理学家进行形成性研究，然后设计TaleBot系统——一个AI赋能的系统，支持儿童根据个人情境共同创作克服日常逆境的故事。在教师指导下，对12名小学生在学校心理咨询室进行系统评估，并对儿童与AI共同创作的故事进行家长反思性访谈。

Result: 研究发现TaleBot鼓励儿童表达情感和想法，为教师提供个性化支持的机会，并帮助家长更好地理解家庭沟通对儿童心理健康的深远影响。系统促进了儿童自我表达和教师、家长对儿童心理健康的理解。

Conclusion: 提出了使用生成式AI支持儿童心理健康教育和干预的设计启示，强调在学校和家庭环境中应用AI技术促进儿童心理韧性的重要性。

Abstract: Resilience is a key factor affecting children's mental wellbeing and future development. Yet, limited HCI research has explored how to help children build resilience through adversarial experiences. Informed by a formative study with elementary school teachers and professional psychologists, we design TaleBot, an AI-empowered system that supports children to co-create stories about overcoming everyday adversities tailored to their personal situations. We evaluated the system with 12 elementary children in school counseling rooms under teacher guidance and conducted reflective interviews with parents upon the Child-AI co-created stories. The findings show that TaleBot encourages children in self-expression of feelings and thoughts, creating opportunities for teachers to provide personalized support and for parents to better understand the profound impact of family communication on children's mental wellbeing. We conclude with design implications for using generative AI to support children's mental health education and interventions across school and family contexts.

</details>


### [145] [FuturePrism: Supporting Adolescence in Collaborative Storytelling to Cope with Future Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23108)
*Yonglin Chen,Pengcheng An,Xueliang Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: FuturePrism是一个基于生成式AI的协作叙事系统，通过三角色扮演机制帮助青少年应对未来生活挑战，显著提升希望水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年常因缺乏执行功能来制定具体路径而对未来不确定性感到焦虑，需要系统支持他们应对未来挑战

Method: 基于Snyder希望理论，采用三角色扮演机制外化认知过程，通过四个叙事章节（目标、机会、挑战、能动性）进行AI辅助的协作叙事

Result: 对20名青少年的评估显示，系统显著提升瞬时希望水平（特别是能动性维度），参与者报告高度叙事沉浸感和积极的系统可用性反馈

Conclusion: AI辅助的协作叙事赋能青少年培养应对未来挑战的积极态度，为青少年心理健康干预提供了有效工具

Abstract: FuturePrism is a GenAI-empowered collaborative storytelling system designed to scaffold adolescents to navigate future life challenges. Adolescents often suffer from anxiety related to future uncertainty for lacking the executive function to develop concrete pathways. Operationalizing Snyder's Hope Theory, the system utilizes a triadic role-play mechanics to externalize cognitive processes through four narrative chapters: The Goal, The Opportunity, The Challenge, and The Agency. An evaluation workshop with 20 adolescents demonstrated that FuturePrism significantly enhances momentary hope levels, particularly in the Agency dimension. Participants reported high levels of narrative immersion and positive feedback towards system usability. Participants also confirmed that the AI-scaffolded collaborative storytelling empowered them to develop positive attitudes towards future challenges.

</details>


### [146] [VRSL:Exploring the Comprehensibility of 360-Degree Camera Feeds for Sign Language Communication in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23265)
*Gauri Umesh Rajmane,Ziming Li,Tae Oh,Roshan Peiris*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索在VR中集成手语，通过身体佩戴的360度摄像头拍摄美国手语视频，测试其可理解性和用户体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在虚拟现实环境中集成手语通信的可行性，以增强聋人和听力障碍用户在虚拟环境中的可访问性和协作能力

Method: 使用身体佩戴的360度摄像头在三个位置（头部、肩部、胸部）录制ASL视频，让10名参与者识别手语符号，评估不同位置的可理解性

Result: 肩部摄像头获得最高准确率（85%），但不同位置间差异无统计学显著性；整体理解成功率为83.3%；参与者指出360度视频的周边失真影响清晰度

Conclusion: 视频基础的ASL通信在VR中具有潜力，但需要优化摄像头角度、减少失真并探索替代安装位置；参与者偏好手语而非文本通信，强调开发此方法对提升DHH用户在虚拟环境中的可访问性和协作的重要性

Abstract: This study explores integrating sign language into virtual reality (VR) by examining the comprehensibility and user experience of viewing American Sign Language (ASL) videos captured with body-mounted 360-degree cameras. Ten participants identified ASL signs from videos recorded at three body-mounted positions: head, shoulder, and chest. Results showed the shoulder-mounted camera achieved the highest accuracy (85%), though differences between positions were not statistically significant. Participants noted that peripheral distortion in 360-degree videos impacted clarity, highlighting areas for improvement. Despite challenges, the overall comprehension success rate of 83.3% demonstrates the potential of video-based ASL communication in VR. Feedback emphasized the need to refine camera angles, reduce distortion, and explore alternative mounting positions. Participants expressed a preference for signing over text-based communication in VR, highlighting the importance of developing this approach to enhance accessibility and collaboration for Deaf and Hard of Hearing (DHH) users in virtual environments.

</details>


### [147] [BRIDGE: Borderless Reconfiguration for Inclusive and Diverse Gameplay Experience via Embodiment Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23288)
*Hayato Saiki,Chunggi Lee,Hikari Takahashi,Tica Lin,Hidetada Kishi,Kaori Tachibana,Yasuhiro Suzuki,Hanspeter Pfister,Kenji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BRIDGE系统通过重建广播视频中的球员3D动作序列，结合体现感知的可视化框架，提升轮椅篮球战术训练的可访问性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残障运动训练资源有限，限制了运动员和教练员接触专项运动动作和战术协调的机会，需要开发更易获取的战术训练工具。

Method: 开发BRIDGE系统：1）重建管道从广播视频检测跟踪球员生成3D比赛序列；2）体现感知可视化框架分解头部、躯干和轮椅基座方向来表示注意力、意图和移动性。

Result: 在20名参与者（10名国家队球员和10名业余球员）的对照研究中，BRIDGE显著提升了球员姿态的自然感知度，使战术意图更易理解，支持功能分级，并改善了参与者的自我效能感。

Conclusion: BRIDGE系统推进了包容性体育学习和可访问教练实践，为残障运动提供了更公平的战术资源获取途径。

Abstract: Training resources for parasports are limited, reducing opportunities for athletes and coaches to engage with sport-specific movements and tactical coordination. To address this gap, we developed BRIDGE, a system that integrates a reconstruction pipeline, which detects and tracks players from broadcast video to generate 3D play sequences, with an embodiment-aware visualization framework that decomposes head, trunk, and wheelchair base orientations to represent attention, intent, and mobility. We evaluated BRIDGE in two controlled studies with 20 participants (10 national wheelchair basketball team players and 10 amateur players). The results showed that BRIDGE significantly enhanced the perceived naturalness of player postures and made tactical intentions easier to understand. In addition, it supported functional classification by realistically conveying players' capabilities, which in turn improved participants' sense of self-efficacy. This work advances inclusive sports learning and accessible coaching practices, contributing to more equitable access to tactical resources in parasports.

</details>


### [148] [Understanding Usage and Engagement in AI-Powered Scientific Research Tools: The Asta Interaction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23335)
*Dany Haddad,Dan Bareket,Joseph Chee Chang,Jay DeYoung,Jena D. Hwang,Uri Katz,Mark Polak,Sangho Suh,Harshit Surana,Aryeh Tiktinsky,Shriya Atmakuri,Jonathan Bragg,Mike D'Arcy,Sergey Feldman,Amal Hassan-Ali,Rubén Lozano,Bodhisattwa Prasad Majumder,Charles McGrady,Amanpreet Singh,Brooke Vlahos,Yoav Goldberg,Doug Downey*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了Asta交互数据集，这是一个包含20多万用户查询和交互日志的大规模资源，用于分析研究人员在真实环境中如何使用AI驱动的科学工具。研究发现用户提交的查询比传统搜索更长更复杂，将系统视为研究合作伙伴，并非线性地使用生成内容作为持久性成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的科学研究工具正快速融入研究流程，但领域缺乏对研究人员在真实环境中如何使用这些系统的清晰了解。需要理解用户如何与AI研究助手互动，以指导未来设计和评估。

Method: 收集并分析Asta交互数据集，包含来自两个已部署工具（文献发现界面和科学问答界面）的20多万用户查询和交互日志。使用该数据集分析查询模式、参与行为以及使用随经验的变化。开发新的查询意图分类法。

Result: 用户提交的查询比传统搜索更长更复杂；将系统视为协作研究伙伴，委托起草内容和识别研究差距等任务；将生成响应视为持久性成果，以非线性方式重新访问和导航输出及引用证据；随着经验增加，用户提出更有针对性的查询并更深入地参与引用证据，但关键词式查询即使在经验丰富的用户中仍然存在。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了研究人员在真实环境中如何使用AI研究助手，提供了对用户行为模式的深入理解。发布匿名数据集和分析结果以及新的查询意图分类法，旨在为未来真实世界AI研究助手的设计提供信息，并支持现实的评估。

Abstract: AI-powered scientific research tools are rapidly being integrated into research workflows, yet the field lacks a clear lens into how researchers use these systems in real-world settings. We present and analyze the Asta Interaction Dataset, a large-scale resource comprising over 200,000 user queries and interaction logs from two deployed tools (a literature discovery interface and a scientific question-answering interface) within an LLM-powered retrieval-augmented generation platform. Using this dataset, we characterize query patterns, engagement behaviors, and how usage evolves with experience. We find that users submit longer and more complex queries than in traditional search, and treat the system as a collaborative research partner, delegating tasks such as drafting content and identifying research gaps. Users treat generated responses as persistent artifacts, revisiting and navigating among outputs and cited evidence in non-linear ways. With experience, users issue more targeted queries and engage more deeply with supporting citations, although keyword-style queries persist even among experienced users. We release the anonymized dataset and analysis with a new query intent taxonomy to inform future designs of real-world AI research assistants and to support realistic evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Complete Robust Hybrid Systems Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22853)
*Noah Abou El Wafa,André Platzer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒微分动态逻辑，用于规范和推理鲁棒混合系统，确保定义的性质在拓扑上开放且对无穷小扰动具有鲁棒性，无需显式误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合系统验证方法通常需要显式指定误差边界，这在实际应用中可能不自然且繁琐。本文旨在开发一种逻辑框架，能够自然地确保系统规范对微小扰动具有鲁棒性，而无需在语法或证明中引入显式的定量误差边界。

Method: 引入鲁棒微分动态逻辑（微分动态逻辑的一个片段），通过语法限制确保定义的性质在拓扑上是开放的，从而对无穷小扰动具有鲁棒性。该方法避免了在语法或证明中引入显式误差边界。

Result: 证明了鲁棒微分动态逻辑对于一般混合系统可达性性质的绝对完备性。这是首个针对具有精确语义的混合系统的绝对完备性证明。证明是构造性的、自包含的，并展示了如何通过证明自动验证鲁棒正确的混合系统可达性规范。

Conclusion: 鲁棒微分动态逻辑为混合系统验证提供了一个强大的理论框架，能够自然地处理鲁棒性要求，无需显式误差边界。该逻辑的绝对完备性证明为自动验证鲁棒正确的混合系统可达性规范提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces robust differential dynamic logic (a fragment of differential dynamic logic) to specify and reason about robust hybrid systems. Practically meaningful syntactic restrictions naturally ensure that definable properties are topologically open and thus by construction robust with respect to infinitesimal perturbations, without explicit quantitative margins of error in the syntax or in proofs.
  The main result is a proof of absolute completeness of robust differential dynamic logic for reachability properties of general hybrid systems. This is the first absolute completeness proof for hybrid systems with exact semantics. The proof is constructive, self-contained, and demonstrates how robustly correct hybrid systems reachability specifications can be automatically verified through proof.

</details>


### [150] [Effectful Toposes and Their Lawvere-Tierney Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23086)
*Rinta Yamada*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文引入effectful toposes作为effective topos的扩展，研究其相对于Lawvere-Tierney拓扑的结构，建立了带效果的计算与相对化可实现性之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带效果的计算模型与可实现性理论之间的联系，将effective topos扩展到包含计算效果的范畴，探索Lawvere-Tierney拓扑在带效果范畴中的对应关系。

Method: 1. 通过提升evidenced frame（最近提出的带效果计算模型）来构造effectful toposes；2. 在effectful toposes上定义Lawvere-Tierney拓扑并刻画其层；3. 证明双否定拓扑的层topos与带CPS效果的effectful topos同构。

Result: 1. 成功构造了effectful toposes作为effective topos的扩展；2. 建立了effectful toposes上Lawvere-Tierney拓扑与计算预言之间的对应关系；3. 证明了带CPS效果的effectful topos与双否定拓扑的层topos同构，为经典可实现性提供模型。

Conclusion: effectful toposes为研究带效果的计算与相对化可实现性之间的联系提供了范畴框架，建立了计算预言与Lawvere-Tierney拓扑的对应，并通过CPS效果与双否定拓扑的同构展示了该框架在经典可实现性建模中的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces effectful toposes as an extension of the effective topos and investigates their structure relative to Lawvere-Tierney topologies. First, we formulate effectful toposes by lifting the evidenced frame, which is a recently proposed model for effectful computation. Next, we define Lawvere-Tierney topologies on effectful toposes and characterize the sheaves on them. In the effective topos, Lawvere-Tierney topologies are known to correspond to computational oracles. Finally, to demonstrate that our result contributes to the connection between realizability relativized by oracles and effects, we show that the sheaf topos for the double negation topology is isomorphic to the effectful topos with the Continuation-Passing-Style effect; these toposes serve as a model of classical realizability.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Communication-Guided Multi-Mutation Differential Evolution for Crop Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22804)
*Sakshi Aggarwal,Mudasir Ganaie,Mukesh Saini*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出DE-MMOGC算法，结合多变异算子引导通信和动态算子选择机制，集成集合卡尔曼滤波处理不确定性，应用于作物模拟模型校准，提高作物产量预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分进化算法在不确定环境下性能受限，作物模拟模型校准面临天气参数不确定性和观测数据缺失的挑战，需要更鲁棒的优化方法提高预测精度。

Method: 提出DE-MMOGC算法：1) 多变异算子引导通信机制促进探索避免早熟收敛；2) 动态算子选择机制选择最佳变异算子；3) 集成集合卡尔曼滤波处理不确定性和缺失观测；4) 应用于WOFOST作物模拟模型，优化温度和降雨等关键天气参数。

Result: DE-MMOGC在作物产量预测中优于传统进化优化器，与真实叶面积指数值相关性更好，证明是作物监测的鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: DE-MMOGC通过多变异算子通信和动态选择机制，结合集合卡尔曼滤波处理不确定性，显著提高了作物模拟模型的校准精度和预测性能，为不确定环境下的优化问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a multi-mutation optimization algorithm, Differential Evolution with Multi-Mutation Operator-Guided Communication (DE-MMOGC), implemented to improve the performance and convergence abilities of standard differential evolution in uncertain environments. DE-MMOGC introduces a communication-guided scheme integrated with multiple mutation operators to encourage exploration and avoid premature convergence. Along with this, it includes a dynamic operator selection mechanism to use the best-performing operator over successive generations. To assimilate real-world uncertainties and missing observations into the predictive model, the proposed algorithm is combined with the Ensemble Kalman Filter. To evaluate the efficacy of the proposed DE-MMOGC in uncertain systems, the unified framework is applied to improve the predictive accuracy of crop simulation models. These simulation models are essential to precision agriculture, as they make it easier to estimate crop growth in a variety of unpredictable weather scenarios. Additionally, precisely calibrating these models raises a challenge due to missing observations. Hence, the simplified WOFOST crop simulation model is incorporated in this study for leaf area index (LAI)-based crop yield estimation. DE-MMOGC enhances the WOFOST performance by optimizing crucial weather parameters (temperature and rainfall), since these parameters are highly uncertain across different crop varieties, such as wheat, rice, and cotton. The experimental study shows that DE-MMOGC outperforms the traditional evolutionary optimizers and achieves better correlation with real LAI values. We found that DE-MMOGC is a resilient solution for crop monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Optimizing SSD-Resident Graph Indexing for High-Throughput Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22805)
*Weichen Zhao,Yuncheng Lu,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Jiehui Li,Minghao Zhao,Yakun Li,Weining Qian*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: VeloANN：一种基于SSD的图近似最近邻搜索系统，通过局部感知数据布局和协程异步运行时缓解存储停顿，在仅使用10%内存的情况下达到内存系统92%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SSD的图近似最近邻搜索系统存在CPU利用率低和读取放大问题，主要原因是图遍历过程中访问局部性有限导致的存储停顿。

Method: 1. 局部感知数据布局：采用分层压缩和亲和性数据放置方案，将相关向量共置在同一页面；2. 记录级缓冲池：按向量邻居分组记录，持久保留热记录；3. 协程异步运行时：轻量级任务调度减少磁盘I/O中断开销；4. 异步预取和波束感知搜索策略：优先使用缓存数据。

Result: VeloANN在吞吐量上比现有基于磁盘的ANN系统提升最高5.8倍，延迟降低3.25倍，在仅使用10%内存的情况下达到内存系统92%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: VeloANN通过创新的数据布局、缓冲池设计和异步运行时，有效解决了基于SSD的图ANN系统中的存储停顿问题，实现了接近内存系统性能的磁盘级解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) methods (e.g., HNSW) have become the de facto state of the art for their high precision and low latency. To scale beyond main memory, recent out-of-memory ANNS systems leverage SSDs to store large vector indexes. However, they still suffer from severe CPU underutilization and read amplification (i.e., storage stalls) caused by limited access locality during graph traversal. We present VeloANN, which mitigates storage stalls through a locality-aware data layout and a coroutine-based asynchronous runtime. VeloANN utilizes hierarchical compression and affinity-based data placement scheme to co-locate related vectors within the same page, effectively reducing fragmentation and over-fetching. We further design a record-level buffer pool, where each record groups the neighbors of a vector; by persistently retaining hot records in memory, it eliminates excessive page swapping under constrained memory budgets. To minimize CPU scheduling overheads during disk I/O interruptions, VeloANN employs a coroutine-based asynchronous runtime for lightweight task scheduling. On top of this, it incorporates asynchronous prefetching and a beam-aware search strategy to prioritize cached data, ultimately improving overall search efficiency. Extensive experiments show that VeloANN outperforms state-of-the-art disk-based ANN systems by up to 5.8x in throughput and 3.25x in latency reduction, while achieving 0.92x the throughput of in-memory systems using only 10% of their memory footprint.

</details>


### [153] [Workload-Aware Incremental Reclustering in Cloud Data Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23289)
*Yipeng Liu,Renfei Zhou,Jiaqi Yan,Haunchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: WAIR是一种工作负载感知的自动重聚类算法，通过识别和仅重聚类边界微分区来优化云数据仓库的查询性能，同时降低重聚类成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云数据仓库依赖元数据（如分区映射）进行高效的数据剪枝，但大规模表中数据聚类的维护在动态云环境中面临挑战。现有自动聚类方法缺乏灵活性，无法适应持续数据摄入和不断变化的工作负载。

Method: 提出将重聚类策略与聚类键选择分离的概念，引入边界微分区（位于查询范围边界的分区），并设计WAIR算法——一种工作负载感知算法，仅识别和重聚类对剪枝效率最关键的那些边界微分区。

Result: WAIR实现了接近完全排序表布局的查询性能，但重聚类成本显著降低，且有理论上限。原型系统在标准基准测试（TPC-H、DSB）和真实工作负载上的评估显示，WAIR相比现有解决方案提高了查询性能并降低了总体成本。

Conclusion: WAIR通过分离重聚类策略与聚类键选择，并专注于边界微分区的智能重聚类，在动态云环境中实现了查询性能与重聚类成本之间的有效平衡，为大规模数据仓库提供了实用的自动聚类解决方案。

Abstract: Modern cloud data warehouses store data in micro-partitions and rely on metadata (e.g., zonemaps) for efficient data pruning during query processing. Maintaining data clustering in a large-scale table is crucial for effective data pruning. Existing automatic clustering approaches lack the flexibility required in dynamic cloud environments with continuous data ingestion and evolving workloads. This paper advocates a clean separation between reclustering policy and clustering-key selection. We introduce the concept of boundary micro-partitions that sit on the boundary of query ranges. We then present WAIR, a workload-aware algorithm to identify and recluster only boundary micro-partitions most critical for pruning efficiency. WAIR achieves near-optimal (with respect to fully sorted table layouts) query performance but incurs significantly lower reclustering cost with a theoretical upper bound. We further implement the algorithm into a prototype reclustering service and evaluate on standard benchmarks (TPC-H, DSB) and a real-world workload. Results show that WAIR improves query performance and reduces the overall cost compared to existing solutions.

</details>


### [154] [AlayaLaser: Efficient Index Layout and Search Strategy for Large-scale High-dimensional Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23342)
*Weijian Chen,Haotian Liu,Yangshen Deng,Long Xiang,Liang Huang,Gezi Li,Bo Tang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AlayaLaser是一个高效的基于磁盘图索引系统，针对大规模高维向量相似性搜索，通过SIMD指令和优化技术解决计算瓶颈而非I/O瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于磁盘的图索引系统普遍关注I/O优化，但随着向量维度增加（数百或数千维），系统性能实际上受计算限制而非I/O限制，这揭示了重要的优化机会。

Method: 1) 使用改进的屋顶线模型分析现有系统性能；2) 设计新颖的磁盘数据布局以缓解计算瓶颈，利用现代CPU的SIMD指令；3) 开发一系列优化技术，包括基于度的节点缓存、基于聚类的入口点选择和早期调度策略。

Result: 在广泛的大规模高维向量数据集上进行实验验证，AlayaLaser不仅超越了现有基于磁盘的图索引系统，而且达到甚至超过了内存索引系统的性能。

Conclusion: 对于大规模高维向量相似性搜索，计算优化比I/O优化更为关键，AlayaLaser通过针对计算瓶颈的系统设计实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: On-disk graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is essential for large-scale, high-dimensional vector retrieval, yet its performance is widely recognized to be limited by the prohibitive I/O costs. Interestingly, we observed that the performance of on-disk graph-based index systems is compute-bound, not I/O-bound, with the rising of the vector data dimensionality (e.g., hundreds or thousands). This insight uncovers a significant optimization opportunity: existing on-disk graph-based index systems universally target I/O reduction and largely overlook computational overhead, which leaves a substantial performance improvement space.
  In this work, we propose AlayaLaser, an efficient on-disk graph-based index system for large-scale high-dimensional vector similarity search. In particular, we first conduct performance analysis on existing on-disk graph-based index systems via the adapted roofline model, then we devise a novel on-disk data layout in AlayaLaser to effectively alleviate the compute-bound, which is revealed by the above roofline model analysis, by exploiting SIMD instructions on modern CPUs.
